Post #1444 · 08-27-2014, 02:02 AM · post_id 132891

Wrote a super quick little story tonight for stress-relief reasons. My  lion siblings, Sally and Max again. Might write a whole little  mini-series of super short nut shots or kneeing stories, and link them  together. But for now, enjoy this one!

Let me know if you like it!

-------------------------------------------------

The First Shower 

-------------------------------------------------

Sally yawned, her golden-furred ears pinned back. It was far too early  for the young lioness to be up. Stupid school. She missed summer  already. Sally rubbed her eyes as she padded down the hallway. The  lioness had only just gotten out of bed and was still dressed in her light-blue PJ's. Her favorite cartoon kitty adorned the front of her  pajama shirt. Her tufted tail flicked behind her as she headed for the  bathroom, ready for a shower.

"Outta the way, runt." Her older brother Max stood at the far end of the hall. "I get the shower first."

"Nuh uh." Sally murmured, still groggy. She stepped in front of the bathroom door. "I was here first."

"Well...I was up first." Max padded towards her, pinning his rounded ears back. "And I'm bigger. And older. So I get it first."

Sally's straw-colored muzzle twisted into a grimace. She didn't have the  time or the patience to deal with her brother's attempts to boss her  around. Not that he usually had much luck. Max might have been older, but he was also a scrawny geek of a lion with only hints of a reddish-black mane coming in around his neck. Glasses perched on his golden-furred muzzle. He only wore a pair of black boxers, and the geeky lion's skinny body hardly looked imposing.

"Get lost, Max." Sally gave him a little shove. "Wait your turn."

"You get lost, ya little brat." Max shoved her back.

Sally stumbled, yelping. "Meanie!" She hissed at him.

Max laughed, shrugging. "That's what you get. Now move it."

"No!" Sally grabbed the door knob.

"Oh no you don't." Max grabbed Sally's hand, twisting it away from the door knob. Okay, maybe he was a 
little
 stronger than his sister. "I'm  taking the first shower. You'll be lucky if I leave you any hot water!"

"No, Max. You're not." Time for her favorite move. Sally smirked at her  brother, then kneed the older lion in the balls as hard as she could.

"AWWWWWHH!" Max made the same silly noise he did whenever she got him in  the boy buttons. His blue eyes popped out behind his glasses. His  muzzle hung open, ears pinned back. Sally felt the young lion's balls  crunch against her knee like a couple of oversized rubber grapes. He  doubled over, face twisting into a grimace of feline agony as he  released her hands to grab his crotch. "Ooooooohhhhhh!"

Sally just giggled at the look on his face. She loved doing that.  Despite the force of the blow, kneeing a guy in the balls was casual as  could be for her. She just lifted her knee, and guys went down. No  biggie. Max wobbled, his eyes rolling back, scrawny knees knocking  together as he cupped his feline privates. Sally counted down in her  head. Three. Two. One.

Max dropped onto his knees right on schedule. Then he flopped forward  onto his face, and curled up on the hallway floor. The nerdy young lion  gave a long, whimpering groan, squirming in pain Sally would never  understand. She giggled at him again. He looked so funny rolling back  and forth, wearing nothing but his boxers and cupping his nards.

"Uhhhn! NNHHHR!" Max couldn't seem to make any comprehensible sounds. "Ooohhhhhhh!"

"Right in the kitten makers, huh Max?" Sally smirked, prodding at his  hands with her bare footpaw. "Do they hurt? Huh? What's it feel like?"

"Shut up!" Max's voice was little more than a pained squeak as he  twisted and rolled on the floor. "Oh, God! Oh! Oooooh, my nards. My  naaaaards!"

Sally cupped her own crotch, scrunching up her muzzle, ears pinned. She  tried to cross her eyes. "My nards, my dumb nards!" The young lioness  giggled, then thrust her knee into the air. "That's what you get, Max.  Mess with girls, you get kneed in the nards."

Max curled up at her feet, groaning. "Mmmrrrhhn! Urrrhhhnnh!" He  uncurled again, pounded a fist against the floor, and then rocked back  and forth before laying on his back, cupping himself in both hands.  "Awwwrrrhhh! Unnhh....my freakin' nards!"

Sally beamed. One lioness knee, two lion nards. Nothing like giving a  good knee in the balls to start her day off right. And she got the  shower. Mission accomplished. She opened the door to the bathroom,  glancing back at her brother. "I'm going to take my shower now. Don't  expect any hot water. Have fun playing with your balls, though."

( This place needs a story lately! So...here's a super, super quick/rough one I just whipped up on the spot. Didn't read it back or edit anything but the one mistake I spotted right away, so don't mind the mistakes and typos! Let me know if you enjoy. ) 

"You gotta do it to the next boy...I dared you!"

"Okay, okay." Sarah giggled, her rounded lioness ears flicked back. "I'll do it." 

"There's one!" The vixen pointed at a chubby, nerdy looking coyote boy emerging from the changing room at the local public pool. "Quick, kick him in the nards!"

Sarah didn't even know who he was. Just some poor coyote dork in the wrong place at the wrong time. He was a little on the pudgy side, with black glasses perched atop his tan furred muzzle. Fur the color of ash covered most of his chubby frame, fading to a sandy tan across his hands and feet. His tan-furred ears perked up at Melissa's words, but before he could even register them, Sarah was already striding towards him, intent on her targets. 

Without another word, the lioness lashed out with her bare, golden furred foot, kicking the young coyote square in the balls. Sarah buried the top of her footpaw in the center of his crotch, the coyote's testicles bulging out against his swimsuit for a moment. Sarah felt them squish a little, like oversized rubber grapes. The impact sounded with a loud 
thwack
 followed immediately by a high pitched canine purely of purely male agony. 

"AAARRRRPPP!" The nerdy coyote doubled over in an instant, eyes bugging out behind his glasses. 

"Oh my god!" Melissa laughed so hard the vixen nearly ended up as bent over as the coyote. "He sounded just like my brother!" 

The coyote soon sagged to his knees, his eyes rolling back, ears flattened against his skull. Cupping his crotch in both tan-furred hands, the pudgy 'yote slowly leaned forward till his muzzle brushed the ground. He sat in that position for a few moments, groaning. "Oooooooooohhhhhhrrrrrrr!" 

Soon, Sarah was laughing just as hard as Melissa. She knew she ought to feel bad about it but dang it, it was just so much fun kicking guys there. They acted like it was the end of the world, all from one little kick. 

"Wanna get him again?" Melissa flashed the lioness a wicked grin, her bushy tail swishing. 

Sarah scrunched her face, tail twitching in opposite time to Melissa's. She knew she shouldn't, but... "Yeah!" 

Melissa hurried to the stricken coyote, and with a little effort, wrenched his hands away, and hauled him partway back up. 

He groaned, squirming weakly against the vixen's grip, shaking his head. "N...No...come on...w..wait..." 

Melissa wriggled with him, giggling. "Grab his hands a sec!" 

Sarah did so, a tense excitement roiling in her belly as she manipulated the unfortunate coyote into position. 

The vixen slipped her hands down the coyote's body, then beamed at Sarah. "Guess what...'yote balls!" Melissa crouched down, yanking the coyote's swim suit down on his ankles. 

Sarah gasped in shock, nearly releasing the coyote. Wide-eyed, she couldn't help but stare. And she could see it all, too. The chubby coyote's gray-furred sheath and ashen-furred testicles were on full display. The plump oval outlines of the nerdy coyote's testicles were clearly silhouetted against his furry scrotum, a bit snug as if trying to protect themselves. 

"Oh my God, look at his stuff!" Melissa laughed and laughed as she stood up and helped hold his arms, peering over his shoulder. "Quick, Sarah, do it! Knee him right in the balls!"

Sarah just couldn't help herself. Whenever Melissa was egging her on, she always ended up doing things she knew she shouldn't. But damn it, it was fun. 

"C'mon, Sarah, do it! Get him, get him! Knee him! Knee him right in his naked, nerdy 'yote balls!" 

Relishing the encouragement, and the wicked thrill, Sarah jammed her knee into the chubby coyote's naked testicles with everything she had. They bulged out against the sack, lifted by her knee. The coyote coyote squealed this time, his little furry eggs jiggling back and forth from the impact. 

"AAAAAAAHHHH!" As soon as the girls released the coyote, he toppled to the floor, curling up in a pudgy heap of relentless male canine anguish. He cupped his balls in both hands, sheath jutting up above them. Groaning, he slowly rocked back and forth, ankles bound by his swimsuit. "Awwwwwww....awwwww! My nards! My naaaaaards, oh, god my frickin' naaaaaards!" Tears leaked out from between squeezed shut eyelids, wetting his scrunched muzzle. 

"Oh yeah, good one Sarah!" Melissa only laughed harder. "You really good him good!"

"Thanks!" Sarah giggled along with her friend, watching the poor coyote squirm in that special pain she'd never understand. She tried not to stare at his exposed sheath. She'd only seen a few of those, before. "Can't believe you pansted him though!"

"Just came to me!" Melissa glanced around, her ears perked in twisted vulpine delight. "Come on, we better go before we get in trouble...let's go back to my place. If Adam's home we can kick him in the boy nuggets too!"

Story time! Was a bit stressed the other night, thought this place could use a new story. So here's my lioness Sarah, teaching her brother Max why he shouldn't pick on his little sister...

Give it back, Max! 

Make me, Brat! 

Just give it! 

Take it then, its right here, cant you reach? 

The sound of sibling squabbles drew Sarahs attention. The young lioness swiveled her ears, listening to her brother and sister argue for a moment. It sounded like Max was picking on their younger sister Sally again. She was getting awfully tired of that. She scrunched her muzzle, growling. 

Everything okay? Her friend Melissas voice rang from the speakers of her laptop, where she was skyping with her. 

Its Max, again. Sally folded her golden-furred arms over her blue pajama top. Ever since he hit his growth spurt this summer, hes acting way too big for his britches.

On the moniter, the vixens eyes went wide. She giggled. Too big for his britches? Thanks, 1940s. 

Sarah giggled, flicking her ears. Ugh, did I really just say that? 

Yes, yes you did. Melissas solemn tone was soon broken up by a giggle of her own. And growth spurt? Since when was a measly inch a growth spurt? 

I know, right? Sarah smiled, scratching her muzzle. If you ask Max, hes practically a giant now. 

A short, skinny giant? Melissa leaned forward in her chair, grinning at the cam.

AgainI know right? And talk to me when his mane comes in. 

Just 
give
 it back, Max! 

Sarah sighed and set her laptop on the bed. She swung her bare, golden furred feet to the floor, blue pj pants swishing. I better go deal with that. 

Alright. Melissa waved. Talk to you in a bit then!

Sarah padded out of her bedroom and into the hall. She swiveled her rounded ears towards the sounds of arguing voices, and followed them towards the living room. At the end of the hall, she saw Max standing behind the arm of the sofa, holding the TV remote up over Sallys head. Her younger brother Max was the middle sibling, and at least a good head taller than their younger sister. Even as she stood on the sofa, stretching for the remote, her tail twitching behind her, she couldnt quite reach it. 

Just as sally almost grabbed the remote, Max passed it to his other hand, laughing. Aww, sucks to be short, huh? 

Shut up, Max!

Sarah grit her teeth, glaring at her brother. Though Max was taller than Sally, he was still a bit of a scrawny runt in his own right. His reddish brown mane was only just starting to come in around his neck in scruffy patches. Wireframe glasses perched on his blunt muzzle. Like the girls, hed changed into PJ pants for the night. His were gray, and he had a black shirt with a video game character on it. Sarah watched him tease his sister for a few moments longer, and then slipped into the living room. Time to knock him down to size.

The lioness walked right up behind Max, and snatched the TV remote out of his hand. Just like he was taller than Sally, Sarah was taller than him. He spun around, his blue eyes wide behind his glasses. Then he gave a little snarl and reached for the remote, only for Sarah to lift it up over his head. He stretched his arms, snatched for it, and Sarah moved it out of reach, smirking at him. 

Give it back, Sarah! Max stretched for the remote

No. Sarah moved it away again. 

Just give it! Max jumped and Sarah pulled it out of reach. 

Thats literally what you said to poor Sally. Sarah passed the remote to her other hand. So what should I say? Oh yeahsucks to be short, huh? 

Max growled, pinning his furry ears back. Thats not funny! 

But it seemed funny to do when you were doing it to your poor sister!

She wouldnt let me watch my show! Max balled up his fists, growling. 

Cause I was watching my show first! Sally hopped up onto the sofa, glaring at him. Then you just took the remote and changed it! 

Tell you what, Max Sarah held the remote up just out of reach. If you can get it without jumping, Ill let you have it back. But then you let Sally watch her show before yours.

Fine, whateverjust give it! Max stretched for the remote again.

Almost there! Sarah held it just a little higher. 

Grunting in effort, Max reached up as high as he could. His fingertips brushed the remote. He stretched his arm to his limit, rising to his tip toes. As soon as he had stretched as far as possible, Sarah smiled and kneed the younger lion in the balls as hard as she could. In a blink her knee was jammed between his legs, the impact lifting his toes from the carpet for a fraction of a second. In that same second, the nerdy lions testicles bulged out against his gray PJs, like rubber grapes squashed between his pelvis and his sisters knee. 

AAAAAAWWWWWWHHHH! 

Maxs hands flew to his crotch as he yowled, his blue eyes popping out behind his glasses. He dropped to the floor in an instant, curling up into a cross-eyed heap of leonine boy pain. His ears pinned back, his fangs were bared in an agonized grimace, and the geeky young lion rolled around on the floor at Sarahs feet. 

Oooooohhh, my baaaalls!

And thats what you get for bullying your sister! Sarah stood over him, grinning.

Sally burst out laughing, bouncing on the sofa. Bahahahahaha! You got him right in the nards! 

Sure did! Sarah thrust her knee into the air as if to demonstrate her technique. Bam! Right there!

Yeah! Sally giggled again, leaning over the back of the sofa to taunt her fallen brother. Right in the nards, Maxy! Girls rule!

Sarah laughed with her sister, nodding. They sure do! She passed the younger lioness the remote. Here. Watch whatever you want. 

I wanna watch Max squirm! Sally set the remote down, watching Max over the back of the sofa. 

The geeky young lion rolled back and forth on the carpet, his face a scrunched mask of pain neither girl would ever understand, let alone have to fear. At first he cupped himself in both hands, squirming. Then he rolled to his back, a forearm draped over his face. He groaned, lifted his knee, then rolled over to his belly, stretched out. His golden fingers protruded under his rump, cradling his no-doubt aching nards. Soon he curled again, once more holding himself in both hands. 

Ooooohh, my balls! Oh, god, my balls! My baaaaaallls!

Sarah giggled and smirked at her younger sister. I think its his balls. She nudged him with her bare foot, giggling. Want another one, Maxy? 

No! Nooo! He swatted at her feet, whining. Go away! 

Make me, Brat. Sarah pushed her foot against his cradling hands. Not so funny to be bullied, is it? She glanced at Sally again. Wanna get him too? 

Yeah! Sally bounced on the sofa cushion. 

Nooooooo! Max whined and curled tighter.

Sarah ignored him. Okay! Come here, Sally. Ill hold him up for you!

As Sally came around the back of the sofa, Sarah reached down and scooped her hands under Maxs arms. The lion wriggled and protested, but Sarah easily hoisted him up onto his feet again. He struggled, and she hooked her arms around his to hold him up, and keep him from covering himself. His tail lashed and bumped her, and she smirked down at the nerdy lion. 

Your kitten makers are getting it now, Maxy. She tugged him back and forth to get him off balance, and keep him from closing up his legs too easily. Kick him in the balls, Sally! Kick him right in the balls!

Sally proved more than happy to do just that. Giggling, the young lioness trotted up and kicked the nerdy lion in the balls. Sally slammed her bare footpaw into Maxs crotch with everything she had, lioness toes mashing soft, already-aching lion testicles. Max jerked in Sarahs arms, shrieking like a little girl. 

AAAAAAAAAAAHHHH! Max thrashed and squirmed, tears building behind his glasses. My nuts! My nuuuuuuuuuts!

Bullseye, Sally! Sarah giggled, keeping the wriggling lion on his feet. 

Totally! Sally giggled and bounced on her feet. I kinda felt em squish! And he went all cross-eyed!

Oooooooooohh! Max gave a deep groan, his head lolling, knees buckling. Oooh, God, my balls, they huuuurt!

Good! Thats what you get for picking on your little sister! Sarah snarled and laughed, tugging Max back and forth. Kick him again, Sally, kick him again! Right in the balls!

Sally didnt hesitate for an instant. She cocked her leg back, and rocketed her foot into the young lions nards hard enough for the 
SMACK
 to resound around the room. Max wailed again, doubling over against his older sisters grip. He twisted and squirmed, his squeal soon giving way to a few drawn out, groaning sobs. 

Ooooooh! Oooh, my balls HRRK! Awww! AwwHRRRNK! Oooh, my balls, my poor balls!

Sarah released his arms, and Max crumpled to the floor between his sisters. He curled up in a tight little ball, fur bristled wherever it stuck out from his clothes. The geeky lion rocked back and forth a few times, moaning about his balls while the girls laughed and laughed. Sally put her foot on him in victorious glee, grinning at her bigger sister. 

Right in the nards! 

Yup, right in his nards alright! Sarah laughed and held out her hand. High five for getting him in the nards!

Sally slapped her hand against her sisters, above the wriggling male. High five! That oughta teach you not to mess with girls, Max! 

Youd think, but Sarah kicked the squirming ball of sobbing lion in the butt. He never really learns. Whaddya think, Sally? Should we let him go? Or should we open him up and get him again?

A young coyote's fuzzy marbles pay the price for gossiping about a girl and her crush... 

 Originally Posted by 
Mythical Pain

  Alex scavenged through his messy locker, searching for a snack. It wasn’t yet lunch time, but the scrawny young coyote’s stomach rumbled, impatient. He needed a snack before gym class. The coyote pinned his tan furred ears back, tossing aside some old granola bar rappers. He was sure there were some unopened bars in there too, buried beneath everything else. Alex tugged a textbook free, shoved it into his backpack, and went back to digging around inside his locker. He flicked his bushy, gray and tail tail. He really needed to clean this thing out. 

“Alex!” 

An angry girl’s voice startled the scrawny young coyote so much he nearly banged his head on his locker. He bolted upright, dropped the granola bar he’d just located, and whirled around. A young lioness his age stood before him, her golden furred arms folded over her chest. The lioness’s cute little rounded ears were flattened back, and her fangs were on display. Her tufted tail flicked back and forth, back and forth. 

“Um, hey, Sally.” Alex rubbed the back of his head, ruffling his fur. She looked mad. Real mad. “What’s up?” 

“Did you tell him?” Sally unfolded her arms, stomping a foot. 

“Did I tell who, what?” Alex scrunched his tan furred muzzle, confused. 

“Adam!” Sally thrust a finger down the hall, towards another locker bay. “Did you tell Adam?” 

That only left Alex more confused. “Did I tell Adam what?” 

“Arrgh!” Sally gave an adorable little snarl, balling up her fists. 

Oooh, she’s really pissed. Alex gulped, wringing his hands, his fluffy tail twitching. He stared at her a moment, racking his brain, but his adolescent coyote mind was soon distracted by the fact the word MEW was spelled out in red letters across her dark blue shirt, right between her breasts. The text seemed to fit almost perfectly between the curves of the young lioness’ breasts. Right between her boobs. God, even when she’s mad she’s cute. 

“Did you tell Adam I said I had a crush on you?” A snarl crept into Sally’s voice, drawing the coyote’s attention away from her chest. 

“What?” He blinked a few times, shaking his head. “No, what I said was-wait, you have a crush on me?” 

“Huh?” Sally’s blue eyes went wide. “I do not! And I can’t believe you told Adam I do!” 

Alex held his hands up, trying to ward off her anger. “No, I told him-”

By then Sally’s foot was already rocketing towards his crotch. The top of the lioness’ shoe made sharp, forceful impact with both young coyote testicles, mashing the furry marbles against his pelvis. The audible thwack hit his ears at the same time the burst of startling, intense pain hit both his fuzzy balls. He doubled over in an instant with a loud, high-pitched canine yelp. 

“AAAARRRRPPPP!” 

For a moment, Alex hung there that way, with his eyes wide and his muzzle gaping as hot pain grew in his young canine nuts. His vision swam as his eyes crossed, and Alex dropped onto his knees, clutching himself in front of the laughing girl. He cradled his nuts in both hands, hunching forward, muzzle scrunching up, ears flattening back. 

“UUNNNNHHH!” Alex made a noise that was half whine, half groan, and flopped over onto the floor. Hot ache radiated from both coyote testicles, and he could scarcely tell which one hurt worse. The way they ached left the scrawny little coyote rolling back and forth on the floor, muzzle twisted up. “Uuuhhhhnnnnn! My nards! Oooooh, my naaaaards!” 

“Ahahahahaha!” Sally laughed and bounced on her toes, then turned to high five a rabbit girl standing nearby. “Bullseye! Right in his ‘yote marbles!” 

“Awwwwwww!” Alex rocked back and forth again, his face a scrunched up mask of purely male pain. 

“Hah, did I get them both?” 

Alex managed a weak nod, rolling onto his side. “Urrrrrrhhhnnnn!” She definitely got them both. “They hurt….so much… Oh my nards, my poor naaards!”

The pain wasn’t new to Alex. At least, not any more. Between Sally, and his older sister Amanda, the geeky young coyote had taken quite a few shots to the puppy makers this year. Yet it never got any easier, and each time it happened, it always seemed to be the worst time all over again. Heedless of the other girls that had gathered to watch him squirm and ask Sally what happened, he felt himself through his pants. One…two. Good. They were both fine, even if they didn’t feel like it.  

Sally leaned over him, smirking. “Good! They’re supposed to hurt! Let’s make sure you’re not faking it! Alyssa, help me out!” 

“What?” Alex gasped, glancing up at the advancing, calico-furred bunny. Before he could say anything else, both girls were prying at his hands. “No, wait, wait, wait!” 

With a little effort, the two girls managed to pull the coyote’s hands away from his crotch. Then they were pulling at his knees, trying to spread his legs. The coyote struggled weakly, trying to wrest his limbs back from the angry females, but it was to little avail. 

“Do it, Sally, do it!” The bunny giggled as she yanked his knee away from its twin. “Step on his balls!” 

“No, Sally, wait, don’t-AWWWWWWHH!” 

The lioness’s sneaker struck home between the young coyote’s slender thighs, colliding with both his aching testicles, and the base of his sheath. As he cried out, the bunny let him go and he curled around Sally’s foot, but by then it was too late. She had his canine privates trapped, and there was little he could do but feel the pain. Gritting her teeth, Sally stepped down hard against helpless coyote testicles. They already-aching glands shrieked their sharp-pain protest to the agonizing pressure, and Alex shrieked with them.

“YAAAAAAHHH! MY NAAAAARDS!” His eyes popped out, trying to escape his skull.

“I hope they really frickin’ hurt, Alex!” Sally leaned in, pressing down harder. She wiggled her foot back and forth, grinding not only his unprotected testicles, but his hidden, uninflated knot, as well. “The whole school thinks I have a crush on you!” 

“You’re squishing them!” Alex shrieked again, prying at her sneaker, his fangs bared in anguished grimace. 

“Good!” Sally grabbed at Alex’s feet and yanked them up into the air. She stood up straight, tugging up on the coyote’s legs. As his hips and tail started to raise, she jammed her foot down even harder, flattening the poor, fuzzy grapes caught in the middle. “How’s this feel, ya big jerk?!” 

“AAAAAAH!” With every passing moment, the ache in the young coyote’s balls grew hotter and more intense, spreading further into his belly. “My baaaaaaaalls!” 

Sally yanked up on the coyote’s feet a few times, matching it with several quick, rhythmic stomps on his canine testicles. The little furry glands shifted and rolled about, but couldn’t escape the lioness’ furry. When she wriggled her foot again, the nerdy coyote nut’s were ground back and forth. Soon the pain was enough to leave tears streaming down Alex’s muzzle. 

“Hah, crybaby pup!” Sally hoisted up her foot and stomped down one last time against his canine nuggets, holding the pressure. “Guys are such wimps about their stupid fuzzy marbles…at least I know you’re not faking it now.” She leaned forward, still stepping on his coyote privates. “Don’t you ever tell Adam or anyone that I have a crush on you again! Not that I do!” She tossed his feet aside, and stepped off of him, huffing. “I definitely don’t!”

“Hhhhhrrrrruuhhnnnnnn!” A long, whimpering groan was the only reply Alex could manage. He curled up on the floor, cradling his unbearably aching grapes. The coyote sucked in a breath, groaned again, and rolled around on the floor. “Ooooohhhhh my baaaaalls!” 

Sally flicked her ears, giggling. “You look like a worm right now…anyway…I feel better. But if I hear you’ve been spreading rumors again? I’m coming to find you for round two! My knee wants to pay a visit to Coyote Ballsville!” She jerked her knee up into the ear, grinning down at him. “Maybe I’ll get you at the bus stop…unless you come up with a really, really good apology by then…” She waggled her fingers at him, then turned away, laughing. “Have fun crawling to gym class…oh, and by the way…it’s dodgeball day…And we all know where everyone aims!” 

Oh, great. Maybe he could skip gym, today. He rolled around on the floor a little more, trying to ignore all the female laughter around him. His balls were still aching relentlessly. Oh, god, why did balls have to hurt so much? As Sally walked up, he glanced up at her, blinking away tears. Her hips swayed a little as she walked, and her tufted golden tail danced above her jeans. 

Even in agony, he couldn’t help but think…

God she had a cute butt.  

When she glanced back and caught him staring, she smirked, waggled her butt, and laughed, walking off. 

Alex sighed. She did have a crush on him, didn’t she? 

A fine way to show it.

Post #1644 · 04-25-2018, 03:23 AM · post_id 145520

This thread needs a serious bump. So, here's my vixen Melissa kicking a dorky cheetah right in the nards! I posted this on SF as well but thought you folks here might appreciate it too! 

***

“Melissa! Melissa!” A familiar voice called out from behind the vixen as she stood at her locker, getting ready to head home from school for the day. “Hey, Melissa!” 

“Just a minute, Tom.” 

Someone tugged her tail. “Hey, Melissa!” 

The vixen flicked her bushy, red-furred tail out of Tom’s grasp. “Seriously, Tom, I’m busy. Just give me a minute!” 

“Okay, fine. One…two…three…four…”

Melissa glanced over her shoulder. Behind her stood a slender, young male cheetah. Evenly spaced black spots covered his golden-beige fur. Glasses perched on his blunt muzzle, with loops around his rounded ears. Ebony tear streaks ran from his eyes, to his mouth. The cheetah’s spotted tail flicked back and forth behind him, a sign of the younger feline’s frequent impatience. He wore a blue t-shirt with stylized teenage warriors from a popular cartoon show, along with khaki shorts that hung down past his knees.

“Five…six…seven…” Tom pushed his glasses up his muzzle, poking his tongue out at Melissa when he saw her looking. “Eight…nine…” 

Melissa sighed, turning her attention back to gathering her things. Tom was one of her geeky brother’s friends, part of their self-described Gathering of Nerds. She liked the scrawny cheetah well enough, and when he was on his own he wasn’t usually too annoying. Though, right now that wasn’t exactly the case. She grit her teeth and flattened back her black-furred ears, then bent forward to pick up her books from the bottom of her messy locker. 

As Melissa bent forward, her tail slipped up between Tom’s legs, wedging itself against the cheetah’s crotch. Tom squeaked, rising to his tip toes behind her. He grabbed at her tail, trying to push it down. 

“Quit making my tail fondle your kitten stuff, ya pervy nerd.” Melissa grabbed the last book and straightened up again, her tail dropping back down. She took her backpack off the hook, and crammed all her textbooks into it before turning around. “What do you want, anyway?” 

 “I…uh…what?” The scrawny cheetah fiddled with his shirt, then adjusted his shorts. “Oh, right. I got a riddle-”

“Tom, did you get a chubby off my tail?” Melissa shouldered her backpack as she turned around, sighing. 

“No!” Tom tugged on his shorts again, wiggling his hips, his spotted tail swishing. “…Maybe.” 

Melissa shook her head, hooking her thumbs under the straps of her backpack. “Frickin’ perv. What did you want, anyway? I don’t really wanna hear a dumb riddle.” 

“Okay, so just…do me a favor.” 

The vixen perked her ears and made a show of glancing towards his crotch. “I don’t care if it did come from my tail, I’m not helping you out with that.” 

“Not that!” Tom laughed and gave up trying to cover up the vague bulge in his shorts. “Even if it is your fault.”

“Uh huh. Look, maybe you nerds like hanging out at school all afternoon, but I wanna get home.”

 In the distance, she saw a few more of her brother’s friends standing around, watching. There was James, a husky with silvery gray, black, and white fur, and Max, a nerdy little lion not much more filled out than Tom, and with a patchy mane. Near them stood Alex, a coyote who was a bit shorter and a year younger than others. She smirked at James and Max and gave them a knowing wave, which they both sheepishly returned. 

“Just spell ‘me’, okay?” Tom giggled and rubbed his spotted hands together. A smug grin spread across his snout. 

Melissa turned her attention back to the cheetah, narrowing her eyes. “What? Why?” 

“Just do it.” Tom bounced on his toes, tail flicking faster. “Please?” 

“Fine, fine.” Melissa adjusted her backpack, spelling out the word. “M. E. Happy?” 

“Nuh huh!” Giggling, Tom licked his nose, beaming up at her. “You forget the D!” 

“What?” Melissa shifted her weight, then hooked her thumbs in her backpack straps again. “I thought you nerds were smart. There’s no D in ‘me’, stupid.” 

“Oh…well…” Tom slowly looked her up and down, then offered her what Melissa imagined the cheetah considered his most debonair smile. His ears perked. “I can fix that, if you want.” 

Melissa stared down at him. He hadn’t. Had he? He had. 

Tom stared back at her, his grin remaining. “Get it? Do ya? D’ya get it?” He bounced on his toes, giggling. “Well?”  

Melissa sent her foot rocketing into Tom’s crotch. The toe of her shoe buried itself in the young cheetah’s testicles, and he doubled over with an agonized yowl, hazel-gold eye popping out and crossing behind his glasses. 

“AWWWWWH!” Tom’s muzzle hung open so far it nearly made an ‘O’ shape, and his ears pinned all the way back, shifting his glasses. For a moment, he hung there like that, bent over, mouth agape. Then his eyes rolled back and he dropped to his knees with a long groan. “Ooooooohhhh, my nards!” The cheetah flopped over onto his side, curling up. He cupped his crotch in both hands, rolling around on the floor. “My nards, my naaaards!” 

The vixen burst out laughing at his expression, and the way he ended up squirming on the hallway floor. “Bullseye. Good luck putting that D in anyone for a while now, ya geeky kitten.” 

Nearby, the other three guys all gave pained groans of sympathy between their own bouts of laughter. Thanks to Melissa and her own friends, all three of the other geeky males present already knew what a good kick in the nards felt like. She was sure they knew how she’d react to a joke like that. Which meant they must have put the cheetah up to it. 

“Awwwwww!” Tom rocked from side to side at her feet, a few tears moistening the fur across his muzzle and his cheeks. His glasses fogged a little. “My nards! Oh, god, my naaards!” 

Melissa watched him squirming, smiling. Tom soon curled up, trembling a little. Then he uncurled, stretching all the way out before rolling to his belly. He pushed up onto his knees, butt and tail in the air, chest on the floor. Then he flopped right back over and returned to wriggling from side to side. The young cheetah’s whole muzzle was scrunched up, fangs bared in pained grimace. As he writhed, he clutched his aching feline privates in both spotted hands. Even his tail was kinked up, as if it too, was suffering that special male agony Melissa would never know. She leaned forward, lowering her voice so that only Tom could hear. 

“Awww, right in the kitten makers, huh buddy?” She moved to stand over him, learning a little closer. “Well, if it makes you feel any better. You look absolutely adorable right now. And you know what I think? Once you can walk again, you should totally nail all three of your friends right in their balls, for putting up to this.” She patted his head. “Only fair, don’t’cha think? Oh, and text me a pic of them rolling around on the floor, afterwards.” 

Melissa straightened up and gave the other boys a wave, then walked away, laughing. No D in me. Tom was right. That was a pretty good joke.

Post #1647 · 10-02-2018, 12:12 AM · post_id 147008

And to keep the thread alive...here's a snippet of another ballbusting story I wrote, featuring my original brother and sister foxes, Melissa and Adam! 

Freshly showered and dressed in cozy pajamas, Melissa made her towards her bedroom. Though the vixen was tired, she wasn’t quite ready to actually try and sleep. Instead, she planned to watch some TV, and chat with her friends. It was early enough in the evening that she had time to lounge in bed a while and still get a good night’s sleep.

The familiar sound of a blaring TV filled the hallway, near her room. Her younger brother Adam had a habit of blasting the volume no matter what he was watching or playing. Tonight, it sounded like some obnoxious anime show. Melissa had already told him to turn it down once, but as usual he didn’t listen. She grumbled, splaying her ears. 

Adam drove Melissa crazy. If it wasn’t a tv show on max volume, it was a video game. If it wasn’t a video game, it was his overly loud skype chats with his friends. Or a noisy sleepover and DnD session. Or something else with a volume setting stuck far beyond eleven. Even worse, she’d caught him snooping in her room a few times recently. Melissa was half-convinced everything he did was just to annoy her. 

May as well go and tell the little brat to turn it down again, she thought. 

Melissa approached Adam’s open door and peered inside. Adam’s TV hung along one wall, across from his desk, where he could easily see it from his bed. Action figures and creature figurines decorated the younger fox’s desk. Gaming consoles with tangled wires sat beneath his TV. Melissa scowled, wishing he’d untangle all that crap before he started a fire. Posters from anime shows, video games, and various canine and feline females in skimpy fantasy armor adorned his walls.

Adam himself was busy making his bed. The younger fox bore a strong resemblance to her, albeit in a shorter, scrawnier frame. He had the same rusty, orange red fur over most of his body. Black highlights tipped his ears and tail, as well as his hands and feet. He wore matching pajamas, a blue shirt and pants both decorated with images of a cartoon dragon. Glasses perched on his muzzle, hooked with loops behind his ears. His bushy tail swished above his rump, with another loop of fabric from his pajamas over the top of it. 

When Adam was bent over, his tail swishing, it tightened his pajamas against his rump. Just under the geeky fox’s butt, two plump, oval shapes outlined themselves against the blue fabric. Melissa fought back a giggle. It looked like her brother was smuggling a couple of large grapes in his pajamas. 

It least that was one important difference between them. She sure as hell didn’t have those. Melissa was awfully glad, too. After all, at times like this, a guy’s balls were just so…vulnerable. And with such a wide open shot presented for her, Melissa just couldn’t resist taking advantage. If he wasn’t going to listen and turn down his TV when she asked the first time? She’d just have to squeeze in a little practice in her favorite sport. 

Kickball. 

Melissa took three quick steps into Adam’s room, and hard as she could, kicked the younger fox in the balls from behind. Bare vixen foot smacked sharply into geeky vulpine testicles. For Melissa, the impact of bare foot in unprotected nuts was a familiar and satisfying feeling, halfway between a squish and a crunch. Adam’s reaction was just as familiar, and just as satisfying. 

“AWWWWWWH!” Adam cried out with the most classic of all the many noises boys made when a girl’s foot found the right spot. 

The nerdy fox’s eyes crossed behind his glasses, and his hands flew from the blankets to his crotch. His knees knocked together, and Adam flopped face-first against his bed. He gave a deep, guttural yet wobbly groan, clutching himself in both hands, half his body hanging off the mattress. His golden-brown eyes rolled back, and with another groan, Adam slowly slid off the bed and onto the floor.  

“Oooooooooh, my baaaaaaalls!” 

Melissa burst out laughing as her bratty younger brother slumped onto the carpet at her feet. He curled up, his ears pinned and his whole muzzle scrunched up. Then Adam started rolling back and forth, his entire red-furred face contorted behind his glasses. That only made Melissa laugh even harder. The rolling around was always her favorite part. It always made her wonder just how getting kicked in the nuts must feel, to make a boy squirm that way. In the end, she was glad she’d never understand the pain. 

“Awww, does it hurt, Adam?” Melissa cupped her own crotch, mocking him. “Ooooh, oooh, ooooooh, my balls!” She waggled her hips, trying to cross her eyes. Then she straightened up, laughing. “I guess it hurts, but I wouldn’t know how it feels. Girls don’t have those!” 

“So…you keep…telling me!” Adam whimpered through clenched teeth, still rolling around at his sister’s feet. “Oooh, God! My freaking balls! Oh, God, my balls!” 

“Your balls, your balls, your silly little balls!” Melissa kicked Adam in the butt when he rolled away from her. “That’s the feeling that proves girls rule, and boys drool!"

There's plenty more story and more ballbusting after that, so if you'd like to read the rest, you can find it here: 

https://www.sofurry.com/view/1372645
